## 1. camera_obj_rel_dist_v1: sb_rowB2_bdc3c2b045ac088766cbef72

Measuring from the closest point of each object, which of these objects (cloth, luggage, kitchen utensils, pillow) is the closest to the camera in frame 6 of 32?

A. cloth
B. luggage
C. kitchen utensils
D. pillow

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 6 of 32.
Step 2: locate the cloth relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 6; the closest point of this object is farther than the kitchen utensils and nearer than the luggage relative to that camera.
Step 3: locate the luggage relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 6; the closest point of this object is farther than the cloth and nearer than the pillow relative to that camera.
Step 4: locate the kitchen utensils relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 6; the closest point of this object is nearer than the cloth relative to that camera.
Step 5: locate the pillow relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 6; the closest point of this object is farther than the luggage relative to that camera.
Step 6: ordered nearest to farthest from the camera viewpoint of frame 6, the objects are kitchen utensils, then cloth, then luggage, then pillow.
So the closest object to that camera viewpoint is the kitchen utensils.
</think>

C

## 2. camera_obj_rel_dist_v1: sb_rowB2_5fcff9f851fcba2a4231f1b9

Measuring from the closest point of each object, which of these objects (ceiling lamp, door, board, bucket) is the closest to the camera in frame 1 of 32?

A. ceiling lamp
B. door
C. board
D. bucket

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 1 of 32.
Step 2: locate the ceiling lamp relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 1; the closest point of this object is farther than the board and nearer than the bucket relative to that camera.
Step 3: locate the door relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 1; the closest point of this object is nearer than the board relative to that camera.
Step 4: locate the board relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 1; the closest point of this object is farther than the door and nearer than the ceiling lamp relative to that camera.
Step 5: locate the bucket relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 1; the closest point of this object is farther than the ceiling lamp relative to that camera.
Step 6: ordered nearest to farthest from the camera viewpoint of frame 1, the objects are door, then board, then ceiling lamp, then bucket.
So the closest object to that camera viewpoint is the door.
</think>

B

## 3. camera_obj_rel_dist_v1: sb_rowB2_5ca0a0a677df1ee15a53a331

Measuring from the closest point of each object, which of these objects (table, sink, tv, sofa) is the closest to the camera in frame 26 of 32?

A. table
B. sink
C. tv
D. sofa

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 26 of 32.
Step 2: locate the table relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 26; the closest point of this object is farther than the sink and nearer than the tv relative to that camera.
Step 3: locate the sink relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 26; the closest point of this object is farther than the sofa and nearer than the table relative to that camera.
Step 4: locate the tv relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 26; the closest point of this object is farther than the table relative to that camera.
Step 5: locate the sofa relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 26; the closest point of this object is nearer than the sink relative to that camera.
Step 6: ordered nearest to farthest from the camera viewpoint of frame 26, the objects are sofa, then sink, then table, then tv.
So the closest object to that camera viewpoint is the sofa.
</think>

D

## 4. camera_obj_rel_dist_v1: sb_rowB2_3ed04b891e3e3384ac7988eb

Measuring from the closest point of each object, which of these objects (sofa, stool, sink, stove) is the closest to the camera in frame 30 of 32?

A. sofa
B. stool
C. sink
D. stove

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 30 of 32.
Step 2: locate the sofa relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 30; the closest point of this object is nearer than the stool relative to that camera.
Step 3: locate the stool relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 30; the closest point of this object is farther than the sofa and nearer than the sink relative to that camera.
Step 4: locate the sink relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 30; the closest point of this object is farther than the stool and nearer than the stove relative to that camera.
Step 5: locate the stove relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 30; the closest point of this object is farther than the sink relative to that camera.
Step 6: ordered nearest to farthest from the camera viewpoint of frame 30, the objects are sofa, then stool, then sink, then stove.
So the closest object to that camera viewpoint is the sofa.
</think>

A

## 7. camera_obj_rel_dist_v2: sb_rowB2_358e61eff7ae486c326493f7

Measuring from the closest point of each object, which of these objects (refrigerator, stove, dishwasher) is the closest to the camera in frame 21 of 32?

A. refrigerator
B. stove
C. dishwasher

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 21 of 32.
Step 2: locate the refrigerator relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 21; the closest point of this object is nearer than the dishwasher relative to that camera.
Step 3: locate the stove relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 21; the closest point of this object is farther than the dishwasher relative to that camera.
Step 4: locate the dishwasher relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 21; the closest point of this object is farther than the refrigerator and nearer than the stove relative to that camera.
Step 5: ordered nearest to farthest from the camera viewpoint of frame 21, the objects are refrigerator, then dishwasher, then stove.
So the closest object to that camera viewpoint is the refrigerator.
</think>

A

## 8. camera_obj_rel_dist_v2: sb_rowB2_13a36428500b9b1b0ce16bbe

Measuring from the closest point of each object, which of these objects (bookshelf, bed, door) is the closest to the camera in frame 17 of 32?

A. bookshelf
B. bed
C. door

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 17 of 32.
Step 2: locate the bookshelf relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 17; the closest point of this object is farther than the bed and nearer than the door relative to that camera.
Step 3: locate the bed relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 17; the closest point of this object is nearer than the bookshelf relative to that camera.
Step 4: locate the door relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 17; the closest point of this object is farther than the bookshelf relative to that camera.
Step 5: ordered nearest to farthest from the camera viewpoint of frame 17, the objects are bed, then bookshelf, then door.
So the closest object to that camera viewpoint is the bed.
</think>

B

## 10. camera_obj_rel_dist_v2: sb_rowB2_16d885535edebe31814ffd46

Measuring from the closest point of each object, which of these objects (stove, dishwasher, fireplace) is the closest to the camera in frame 21 of 32?

A. stove
B. dishwasher
C. fireplace

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 21 of 32.
Step 2: locate the stove relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 21; the closest point of this object is nearer than the dishwasher relative to that camera.
Step 3: locate the dishwasher relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 21; the closest point of this object is farther than the stove and nearer than the fireplace relative to that camera.
Step 4: locate the fireplace relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 21; the closest point of this object is farther than the dishwasher relative to that camera.
Step 5: ordered nearest to farthest from the camera viewpoint of frame 21, the objects are stove, then dishwasher, then fireplace.
So the closest object to that camera viewpoint is the stove.
</think>

A

## 11. camera_obj_rel_dist_v3: sb_rowB2_9d4be7384710c41deb8e7995

Measuring from the closest point of each object, which of these objects (toilet, chair) is the closest to the camera in frame 32 of 32?

A. toilet
B. chair

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 32 of 32.
Step 2: locate the toilet relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 32; the closest point of this object is farther than the chair relative to that camera.
Step 3: locate the chair relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 32; the closest point of this object is nearer than the toilet relative to that camera.
Step 4: ordered nearest to farthest from the camera viewpoint of frame 32, the objects are chair, then toilet.
So the closest object to that camera viewpoint is the chair.
</think>

B

Measuring from the closest point of each object, which of these objects (sink, bathtub) is the closest to the camera in frame 21 of 32?

A. sink
B. bathtub

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 21 of 32.
Step 2: locate the sink relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 21; the closest point of this object is nearer than the bathtub relative to that camera.
Step 3: locate the bathtub relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 21; the closest point of this object is farther than the sink relative to that camera.
Step 4: ordered nearest to farthest from the camera viewpoint of frame 21, the objects are sink, then bathtub.
So the closest object to that camera viewpoint is the sink.
</think>

A

## 14. camera_obj_rel_dist_v3: sb_rowB2_b2ba7dac5e58bcc5b5c69c27

Measuring from the closest point of each object, which of these objects (bathtub, cabinet) is the closest to the camera in frame 31 of 32?

A. bathtub
B. cabinet

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 31 of 32.
Step 2: locate the bathtub relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 31; the closest point of this object is nearer than the cabinet relative to that camera.
Step 3: locate the cabinet relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 31; the closest point of this object is farther than the bathtub relative to that camera.
Step 4: ordered nearest to farthest from the camera viewpoint of frame 31, the objects are bathtub, then cabinet.
So the closest object to that camera viewpoint is the bathtub.
</think>

A

## 16. obj_obj_relative_pos_lr: sb_rowB2_cba9911a7e5d843819e7d244

In frame 17 of 32, relative to table, is chair to the [Left/Right]?

A. Left
B. Right

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 17 of 32.
Step 2: locate the chair relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 17; the centre of this object is to the left of that camera's forward axis.
Step 3: locate the table relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 17; the centre of this object is to the right of that camera's forward axis.
Step 4: from the camera viewpoint of frame 17, the centre of the chair is to the left of the centre of the table.
So the centre of the chair is left relative to the centre of the table.
</think>

A

## 17. obj_obj_relative_pos_lr: sb_rowB2_2fbae6df2ebd920d37731ba3

In frame 13 of 32, relative to washer, is bathtub to the [Left/Right]?

A. Right
B. Left

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 13 of 32.
Step 2: locate the bathtub relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 13; the centre of this object is to the left of that camera's forward axis.
Step 3: locate the washer relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 13; the centre of this object is to the right of that camera's forward axis.
Step 4: from the camera viewpoint of frame 13, the centre of the bathtub is to the left of the centre of the washer.
So the centre of the bathtub is left relative to the centre of the washer.
</think>

B

## 18. obj_obj_relative_pos_lr: sb_rowB2_d028b96cf21c8000a65491c1

In frame 4 of 32, relative to stool, is fireplace to the [Left/Right]?

A. Left
B. Right

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 4 of 32.
Step 2: locate the fireplace relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 4; the centre of this object is to the left of that camera's forward axis.
Step 3: locate the stool relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 4; the centre of this object is to the left of that camera's forward axis.
Step 4: from the camera viewpoint of frame 4, the centre of the fireplace is to the left of the centre of the stool.
So the centre of the fireplace is left relative to the centre of the stool.
</think>

A

## 21. obj_obj_relative_pos_nf: sb_rowB2_d5eecf13599ed8b529f7223a

In frame 17 of 32, relative to the camera, is cabinet [Near/Far] compared to sofa?

A. Near
B. Far

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 17 of 32.
Step 2: locate the cabinet relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 17; the closest point of this object is farther than the sofa relative to that camera.
Step 3: locate the sofa relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 17; the closest point of this object is nearer than the cabinet relative to that camera.
Step 4: from the camera viewpoint of frame 17, the closest point of the sofa is nearer than the closest point of the cabinet.
So the closest point of the cabinet is farther than the closest point of the sofa relative to that camera viewpoint.
</think>

B

In frame 32 of 32, relative to the camera, is tv [Near/Far] compared to shelf?

A. Far
B. Near

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 32 of 32.
Step 2: locate the tv relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 32; the closest point of this object is farther than the shelf relative to that camera.
Step 3: locate the shelf relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 32; the closest point of this object is nearer than the tv relative to that camera.
Step 4: from the camera viewpoint of frame 32, the closest point of the shelf is nearer than the closest point of the tv.
So the closest point of the tv is farther than the closest point of the shelf relative to that camera viewpoint.
</think>

A

## 25. obj_obj_relative_pos_nf: sb_rowB2_ccab2d795c086a30b99a7c9e

In frame 9 of 32, relative to the camera, is bed [Near/Far] compared to cabinet?

A. Far
B. Near

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 9 of 32.
Step 2: locate the bed relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 9; the closest point of this object is nearer than the cabinet relative to that camera.
Step 3: locate the cabinet relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 9; the closest point of this object is farther than the bed relative to that camera.
Step 4: from the camera viewpoint of frame 9, the closest point of the bed is nearer than the closest point of the cabinet.
So the closest point of the bed is nearer than the closest point of the cabinet relative to that camera viewpoint.
</think>

B

In frame 26 of 32, relative to bathtub, is sink to the [Up/Down]?

A. Down
B. Up

Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 26 of 32.
Step 2: locate the sink relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 26; the centre of this object is below that camera's level horizon.
Step 3: locate the bathtub relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 26; the centre of this object is below that camera's level horizon.
Step 4: in frame 26's level horizon, the centre of the sink is higher than the centre of the bathtub.
So the centre of the sink is up relative to the centre of the bathtub.
</think>

B

## 28. obj_obj_relative_pos_ud: sb_rowB2_6c5bb4461186826b9243a9fe

In frame 27 of 32, relative to cabinet, is sink to the [Up/Down]?

A. Down
B. Up

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 27 of 32.
Step 2: locate the sink relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 27; the centre of this object is below that camera's level horizon.
Step 3: locate the cabinet relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 27; the centre of this object is below that camera's level horizon.
Step 4: in frame 27's level horizon, the centre of the sink is higher than the centre of the cabinet.
So the centre of the sink is up relative to the centre of the cabinet.
</think>

B

A. Down

Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 16 of 32.

Step 3: locate the fireplace relative to the camera viewpoint of frame 16; the centre of this object is below that camera's level horizon.
